9c3308e0ecb9b6db41acaf454f011f86b80448ff: $$P(X = k) = \frac{\dbinom{K}{k} \dbinom{N - K}{n - k}}{\dbinom{N}{n}},

cc68a79f65a60fd2da0452fd3904cd611f0c05ad: $$P(X = k) = \binom{n}{k} p^k (1-p)^{n-k}$$
